## leader_desc_commander

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_desc_commander&lang=en)

### 中文

§E§H指挥官§!可以被指派去指挥一支§H舰队§!或§H陆军§!，或是督管一颗§H行星§!，同时还能占据一个生效的§H内阁席位§!§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:13`

### English

§E§HCommanders§! can be assigned to command a §HFleet§! or an §HArmy§!, or govern a §HPlanet§!, and occupy a valid §HCouncil Position§! at the same time§!.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:15`

## RECRUIT_LEADER_AT_CAP_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ARECRUIT_LEADER_AT_CAP_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

招募这名领袖会超出您的领袖上限，增加所有领袖的维护费并减少经验获取。\n\n花费$COST|Y$\n每月维护费§Y[Root.GetUpkeep]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:33`

### English

Recruiting this leader will exceed your Leader Cap, increasing upkeep costs and reducing experience gains for all your leaders.\n\nCost $COST|Y$.\nMonthly upkeep §Y[Root.GetUpkeep]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:39`

## RECRUIT_LEADER_AT_CAP_DESC_NO_UPKEEP

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ARECRUIT_LEADER_AT_CAP_DESC_NO_UPKEEP&lang=en)

### 中文

招募这名领袖会让您超出领袖上限，对所有领袖的维护费和经验值获取产生惩罚效应。\n\n这名领袖将花费$COST|Y$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:34`

### English

Recruiting this leader will exceed your Leader Cap, increasing upkeep costs and reducing experience gains for all your leaders.\n\nCost $COST|Y$.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:40`

## RECRUIT_LEADER_ALREADY_OVER_CAP_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ARECRUIT_LEADER_ALREADY_OVER_CAP_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

您已经超过领袖上限。招募这名领袖会进一步提升您领袖的维护费及降低经验值获取。\n\n花费$COST|Y$。\n每月维护费§Y[Root.GetUpkeep]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:36`

### English

You have exceeded the Leader Cap. Recruiting this leader will further increase upkeep costs and reduce experience gains for all leaders.\n\nCost $COST|Y$.\nMonthly upkeep §Y[Root.GetUpkeep]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:42`

## COUNCIL_LEGITIMACY_TOOLTIP_EFFECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACOUNCIL_LEGITIMACY_TOOLTIP_EFFECT&lang=en)

### 中文

§H$COUNCIL_LEGITIMACY$§!将会基于全国的派系支持度进而影响内阁制定议程的能力。\n\n当前$COUNCIL_LEGITIMACY$：$LEGITIMACY|Y%$\n\n§E效果：§!$TABBED_NEW_LINE$$MOD_COUNCIL_AGENDA_PROGRESS_SPEED$：$AGENDA_SPEED$

### English

Based on Faction Approval throughout the Empire, §H$COUNCIL_LEGITIMACY$§! affects the ability of the Council to enact Agendas.\n\nCurrent $COUNCIL_LEGITIMACY$: $LEGITIMACY|Y%$\n\n§EEffects:§!$TABBED_NEW_LINE$$MOD_COUNCIL_AGENDA_PROGRESS_SPEED$: $AGENDA_SPEED$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:56`

## COUNCIL_LEGITIMACY_TOOLTIP_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACOUNCIL_LEGITIMACY_TOOLTIP_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§G点击开始以选择的设置自动托管该舰队。§!\n§G£shift£-点击将保存该设置至该类型舰船。这不适用于目前已托管的船舶。§!\n§G£ctrl£-点击将保存该设置至所有该类型舰船。这将覆写其自动托管设置。§!

### English

§GClick to begin automating this fleet with the selected settings.§!\n§G£shift£-click to also save them for this ship type. This will NOT apply to ships that are currently automated.§!\n§G£ctrl£-click to apply them to all ships of this type. This will overwrite their automation settings.§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:131`

## egalitarian1_catch_phrase

## materialist1_catch_phrase

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amaterialist1_catch_phrase&lang=en)

## materialist2_catch_phrase

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amaterialist2_catch_phrase&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“万物始于修建之时，不是吗？万物总有修理之日，不是吗？我恰好对这两项工作都有兴趣！”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:608`

### English

## pacifist1_catch_phrase

### 中文

## spiritualist2_catch_phrase

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspiritualist2_catch_phrase&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“星海中蕴藏着无穷的知识，有的被隐藏，有的被埋葬，还有的则被遗忘。我们必须保护这些旧日的真理。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:614`

## skrand_catch_phrase

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Askrand_catch_phrase&lang=en)

§L“你的敌人将尝斯卡拉维德之怒。第一利爪号将再度翱翔于天际！”§!

## sadtoid_catch_phrase

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asadtoid_catch_phrase&lang=en)

### 中文

§L“生命是一种需要珍惜的东西。是一种罕见的意外，是一份珍贵的宝藏。让我们帮助生命的传播，让万物再次欣欣向荣。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:617`

### English

§L"Life is something to cherish. A rare occurrence; a true treasure. Let us help it spread and bloom again."§!

## keides_catch_phrase

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Akeides_catch_phrase&lang=en)

§L“对！没有时间可以浪费，让我去探索星海中的种种奥秘吧。我的舰船在哪呢？”§!

§E超出你的§H$CLASS$容量§!将会对§H$UPKEEP$§!和你所有$CLASS_PLURAL$§H经验§!获取造成递增的大幅惩罚。§H$CLASS$容量§!可以通过解锁特定§H传统§!、§H科技§!或改革§H政府权力制度§!来增加§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:721`

### English

§EExceeding your §H$CLASS$ Capacity§! will add increasingly severe penalties on §H$UPKEEP$§! and §HExperience§! gain for all your $CLASS_PLURAL$. §H$CLASS$ Capacity§! can be increased by unlocking certain §HTraditions§!, §HTechnologies§! or by reforming your §HGovernment Authority§!§!.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:780`

## LEADER_CAPACITY_PENALTY

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ALEADER_CAPACITY_PENALTY&lang=en)

### 中文

由于超出$CLASS$容量，你的$CLASS_PLURAL$的维护费增加$UPKEEP$，$CLASS_PLURAL$获得的经验减少$EXPERIENCE$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:723`

### English

The upkeep of your $CLASS_PLURAL$ is increased by $UPKEEP$, and $CLASS_PLURAL$ gain $EXPERIENCE$ less experience due to being over your $CLASS$ Capacity.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:782`

## decision_geologist_prospect_effect

## decision_geologist_prospect_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_geologist_prospect_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过扫描整个地表并派遣科考队，我们可以寻找未被发现的隐藏财富。这一决议给予我们寻找到额外矿物贮藏的机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:872`

### English

By scanning the entire surface and sending exploration teams, we can search for untapped and hidden riches. This decision gives us a chance to find additional deposits.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:935`

## leader_trait_kidnapper_effect

### 中文

### English

## leader_trait_educator_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_educator_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

知识和经验是每一个伟大社会得以构建的根基。这位领袖知道这些的价值，想尽一切办法在其他人身上倡导和鼓励价值观。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1257`

### English

Knowledge and experience are the foundation of every great society. This leader possesses these values and does everything to promote and encourage them in others.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1334`

## leader_trait_master_bureaucrat

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_master_bureaucrat&lang=en)

## leader_trait_master_bureaucrat_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_master_bureaucrat_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

浸润官场多年，这位领袖已经成长为一个处理文山会海游刃有余的专家。万事万物皆能妥善应对，下属能够腾出更多的时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1259`

### English

Having toiled as a bureaucrat, this leader is a master of paperwork. With everything correctly filed, more time can be devoted to their subordinates.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1336`

## leader_trait_shroud_preacher_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_shroud_preacher_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虚境环抱着每一个活着的生物。能够和个中能量连接，这位领袖对于其麾下追随者可谓尽心尽力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1263`

### English

The Shroud encompasses every living being. Connected to the powers within, this leader fosters true devotion in their followers.

## leader_trait_master_diplomat

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_master_diplomat&lang=en)

### 中文

外交大师

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1264`

### English

Master Diplomat

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1341`

## leader_trait_master_diplomat_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_master_diplomat_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在海外的大使馆与外交官们共事多年，这位领袖知道如何在礼仪上把握文化上的细微差别，高超地与邻近帝国的高级官员保持联络网。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1265`

### English

After many years working with diplomats in foreign embassies, this leader knows how to navigate cultural nuances, masterfully managing a network of high-ranking officials from neighboring empires.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1342`

## leader_trait_supreme_organizer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_supreme_organizer&lang=en)

### 中文

幕后主使

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1266`

### English

Gray Eminence

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1343`

## leader_trait_supreme_organizer_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_supreme_organizer_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过对国家事务的严格控制，这位领袖坐落于一套由错综复杂的规则、法律和官僚流程组成的迷宫的中央，一切设计都是为了提高自己的权力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1267`

### English

With strict control over matters of state, this leader resides at the center of a labyrinthian set of rules, laws, and bureaucratic procedures, all designed to enhance their own power.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1344`

冷酷开发者

### English

Ruthless Developer

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1347`

## leader_trait_arbiter_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_arbiter_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位领袖大打铁腕手腕和对安全法规的完全漠视两张牌，以预期寿命下降和偶发大规模罢工为代价，创造了空前的成果和产出。然而，只要镇压得当，这种微不足道的困难很容易解决。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1271`

### English

With an iron hand and complete indifference toward safety regulations, this leader drives output at the cost of lowering life expectancy and the occasional mass strike. However, with the right amount of force, such nuisances are easily dealt with.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1348`

## leader_trait_automation_champion

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_automation_champion&lang=en)

### 中文

自动化先锋

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1274`

### English

Champion of Automation

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1351`

## leader_trait_automation_champion_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_automation_champion_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

优化对这位领袖至关重要。而自动化便是关键，一切都可以自动化。无论是通过先进的机器人还是更简单的机器，任何途径都将被探索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1275`

### English

Optimization is of paramount importance to this leader. Automation is the key, and everything can be automated. Be it through advanced robotics or simpler machinery, no avenue will be left unexplored.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1352`

## leader_trait_expert_survivalist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_expert_survivalist&lang=en)

### 中文

生存主义专家

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1276`

### English

Expert Survivalist

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1353`

## leader_trait_expert_survivalist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_expert_survivalist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有些个体非常熟练于生存艺术。这位领袖知道如何在最恶劣、最不适宜居住的条件下坚持生活，让新定居者茁壮成长。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1277`

### English

Some individuals are highly skilled in the art of survival. This leader knows how to cling to life even in the harshest, most inhospitable conditions, allowing new settlements to thrive.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1354`

## leader_trait_utopian_idealist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_utopian_idealist&lang=en)

### 中文

乌托邦理想家

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1278`

### English

Utopian Idealist

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1355`

## leader_trait_utopian_idealist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_utopian_idealist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

许多人都梦想构筑一个真正的乌托邦社会。这位领袖将梦想化为了现实。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1279`

### English

Many dream of building a truly utopian society. This leader works to turn those dreams into a reality.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1356`

## leader_trait_mediator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_mediator&lang=en)

### 中文

异种调停者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1280`

### English

Xeno-Mediator

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1357`

## leader_trait_mediator_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_mediator_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一位技术高超的调解员对于高度多样化的人口中心至关重要。这位领袖知道如何化解紧张局势，高效率地管理一个多物种混居的殖民地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1281`

### English

A skilled mediator is essential in highly diverse population centers. This leader knows how to defuse tension and efficiently manage a multi-species colony.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1358`

## leader_trait_mediator_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_mediator_effect&lang=en)

## leader_trait_efficient_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_efficient_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于出生于上层社会，这位领袖对于自身价值有着强烈的信念，并使其致力于维护上层阶级。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1284`

### English

Born into the higher echelons of society, this leader has a strong sense of their own worth, and tends to favor peers of the upper classes.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1361`

## leader_trait_galvanizer

激人奋进

## leader_trait_galvanizer_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_galvanizer_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

拥有强烈的目标感会带来诸多好处。这位领袖知道如何管理民众，给他们一个共同的目标，让他们精力充沛地去追求。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1287`

### English

Having a strong sense of purpose leads to many positive outcomes. This leader knows how to handle the populace, giving them a common goal to pursue with energy.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1364`

## leader_trait_great_researcher

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_great_researcher&lang=en)

### 中文

大研究者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1289`

### English

Great Researcher

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1366`

## leader_trait_great_researcher_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_great_researcher_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在某些罕见的情况下，个体的产出甚至能够超过一整个研究团队。在实验设计、新解决方案和新科技方面，这样的领袖是真正的天才。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1290`

### English

In rare cases, a single person can outperform an entire team of researchers. When it comes to experimental designs, new solutions, and new technologies, this leader is a true genius.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1367`

## leader_trait_deep_space_explorer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_deep_space_explorer&lang=en)

### 中文

深空探险家

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1291`

### English

Deep Space Explorer

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1368`

## leader_trait_deep_space_explorer_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_deep_space_explorer_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

深空探索官员的工作是调查星系最遥远的角落。这位领袖就是这样一名探险家，在最先进科技的加持下，迈向未知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1292`

### English

Deep space exploration officers are assigned to survey the remotest corners of the galaxy. This leader is one such explorer, equipped with state-of-the-art technology to propel them into the unknown.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1369`

## leader_trait_xeno_cataloger

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_xeno_cataloger&lang=en)

### 中文

异种编目家

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1293`

### English

Xeno Cataloger

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1370`

## leader_trait_xeno_cataloger_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_xeno_cataloger_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位领袖负责发现、鉴别、并编目异种生命。获得数据之后将进行评估，以辨别出可能的威胁和未来的污染方案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1294`

### English

This leader is tasked with discovering, identifying, and cataloging all xeno lifeforms. The data is then assessed to determine any possible threats and future contamination protocols.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1371`

## leader_trait_xeno_cataloger_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_xeno_cataloger_effect&lang=en)

$t$完成调查时：\n$tt$获得3个月产量的£unity£§Y$unity$§!

### English

$t$On Completing Survey:\n$tt$Gain 3 months of £unity£§Y$unity$§! production

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1372`

## leader_trait_frontier_adventurer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_frontier_adventurer&lang=en)

### 中文

边疆冒险家

### English

Frontier Adventurer

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1373`

## leader_trait_frontier_adventurer_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_frontier_adventurer_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

探索新星系、寻找新星球、发现被遗忘的文明是一位真探险家所孜孜追求的。这位领袖正是这种理念的具现，总是在行动着寻找丢失的知识或被遗忘的废墟。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1297`

### English

Exploring new systems, finding new worlds, and discovering long-forgotten civilizations are the main concerns of any true adventurer. This leader is the very embodiment of this ideal, always on the lookout for lost knowledge or forgotten ruins.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1374`

## leader_trait_frontier_adventurer_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_frontier_adventurer_effect&lang=en)

### 中文

$t$完成调查时：\n$tt$获得3个月产量的£society£§Y$society_research$§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1298`

### English

$t$On Completing Survey:\n$tt$Gain 3 months of £society£§Y$society_research$§! production

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1375`

## leader_trait_brilliant_shipwright

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_brilliant_shipwright&lang=en)

### 中文

卓越造船家

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1299`

### English

Brilliant Shipwright

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1376`

## leader_trait_brilliant_shipwright_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_brilliant_shipwright_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位领袖在造船领域有广博的知识和天生的理解，不仅负责新科技研发，还监管着我们的船坞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1300`

### English

With extensive knowledge and an innate understanding of shipbuilding, this leader is responsible not only for new technology but also for overseeing our shipyards.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1377`

## leader_trait_truth_seeker

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_truth_seeker&lang=en)

### 中文

真理探求者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1301`

### English

Truth Seeker

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1378`

## leader_trait_truth_seeker_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_truth_seeker_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

追求真理就是打开自己的思想，接受超越眼界的可能性；甚至超越现实本身。这位领袖有一个强烈的愿望，无论有多大的风险都要揭开宇宙的每一个秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1302`

### English

To pursue the truth is to open one's mind to possibilities beyond the horizon; perhaps beyond the veil of reality itself. This leader has a deep desire to uncover every secret of the universe, no matter the risks.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1379`

## leader_trait_surveyor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_surveyor&lang=en)

### 中文

行星分析师

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1303`

### English

Planetary Analyst

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1380`

## leader_trait_surveyor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_surveyor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位领袖发展了调查科技和机械化所需的大规模田野调查经验，将行星调查变成了一门艺术。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1304`

### English

Advanced surveying techniques and machinery demand wide-ranging field experience. This leader's expertise has turned planetary surveying into an art form.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1381`

### 中文

天生工程师

### English

Natural Engineer

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1383`

## leader_trait_repair_crew_effect

$SYSTEM_EFFECTS_TXT$：\n$t$星系内的友军舰船：\n$tt$每月维修船体值：§G+10%§!

## leader_trait_repair_crew_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_repair_crew_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位领袖天生就擅长将损坏的物品修复如初，基本上是在工程舱长大成人的。这些天资使其成为即使没有合适的造船厂也能维护舰队的专家。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1308`

### English

A natural at mending broken things, this leader practically grew up in the engineering bay. Their aptitude makes them an expert in maintaining fleets, even without a proper shipyard.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1385`

## leader_trait_robotist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_robotist&lang=en)

### 中文

机器人之王

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1309`

### English

Bot Lord

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1386`

## leader_trait_robotist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_robotist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位领袖是机器人设计和制造流程领域的大家，长期致力于挑战可能性的极限。机器人业内几乎所有的进展都与这位个体有关。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1310`

### English

This leader is a champion of robot design and manufacturing processes, always pushing the boundaries of what is possible. If there are advancements in robotics at hand, this individual likely had something to do with it.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1387`

## leader_trait_knowledge_for_the_masses

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_knowledge_for_the_masses&lang=en)

### 中文

致力教育家

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1311`

### English

Driven Educator

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1388`

## leader_trait_knowledge_for_the_masses_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_knowledge_for_the_masses_effect&lang=en)

### English

$t$Research per Pop: §G+$@trait_knowledge_masses_research_amt$§!

## leader_trait_knowledge_for_the_masses_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_knowledge_for_the_masses_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

没有什么比适当的教育更重要的了。这位领袖不遗余力地培养着年轻的头脑，并得到了一大批毕业生的帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1313`

### English

Nothing is more important than a proper education. This leader spares no effort to cultivate young minds, aided by a devoted following of graduates.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1390`

## leader_trait_fanatic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_fanatic&lang=en)

### 中文

战狂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1315`

### English

Fanatic Warlord

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1392`

## leader_trait_fanatic_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_fanatic_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位舰队司令狂热地想摧毁敌人，为此几乎陷入了疯狂。虽然在制造死亡方面非常有效，但其鲁莽常常导致双方的大量伤亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1316`

### English

Possessed by a fanatical urge to destroy their enemies in battle, this admiral can approach a state of madness. Though highly effective in dealing out death, their recklessness often leads to high casualties on both sides.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1393`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_reverse_engineer&lang=en)

科技拾荒者

### English

Tech Scavenger

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1394`

$NAVAL_BATTLE_WON_TXT$：\n$t$偷取科技的概率：§Y33%§!\n$t$初始研究进度：§G+50%§!\n$t$可用分类：\n$tt$£engineering_research£ §Y推进力学§!\n$tt$£physics_research£ §Y粒子物理§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1395`

## leader_trait_reverse_engineer_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_reverse_engineer_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位海军上将总是热衷于剖析新的和未知的技术，拥有广泛的知识和灵活的思维，经常在遇到更先进的对手时在岗学习。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1319`

### English

Always keen on dissecting new and unknown technology, this admiral possesses broad knowledge and a flexible mind, often learning on the job when encountering more advanced opponents.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1396`

## leader_trait_supreme_admiral

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_supreme_admiral&lang=en)

### 中文

无敌舰队指挥官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1320`

### English

Armada Commander

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1397`

## leader_trait_supreme_admiral_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_supreme_admiral_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位舰队司令在后勤和战略策划方面表现杰出，能指挥规模远胜过其他同僚的舰队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1321`

### English

Excelling at logistic and strategic planning, this admiral is able to command fleets that would overburden their lesser peers.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1398`

## leader_trait_armorer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_armorer&lang=en)

### 中文

天才军械师

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1322`

### English

Genius Armorer

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1399`

## leader_trait_armorer_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_armorer_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位领袖发明了加固装甲的新方法，很少有武器能穿透根据[Root.GetHisHer]的设计建造的船只。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1323`

### English

This leader has devised new ways to harden armor. There are few weapons in the galaxy that can penetrate the hulls of ships constructed with [Root.GetHisHer] designs.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1400`

## leader_trait_military_overseer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_military_overseer&lang=en)

### 中文

军事监督者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1324`

### English

Military Overseer

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1401`

## leader_trait_military_overseer_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_military_overseer_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

组织海军是一项艰巨的任务，需要仔细的规划以及各项完美的标准。这位领袖的宝贵经历是建立一支高效的海军的无价财富。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1325`

### English

Naval organization is a difficult task, requiring careful planning and impeccable standards. This leader's experience is an invaluable asset in building an efficient navy.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1402`

## leader_trait_master_gunner

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_master_gunner&lang=en)

### 中文

枪炮大师

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1326`

### English

Master Gunner

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1403`

## leader_trait_master_gunner_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_master_gunner_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位舰队司令高度专注于武器发展，时刻保持下属以及武器处于最佳状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1327`

### English

An admiral with a heavy focus on weapons development, they always keep both their person and their guns in pristine working order.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1404`

## leader_trait_demolisher_destiny

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_demolisher_destiny&lang=en)

### 中文

帝国之锤

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1328`

### English

Hammer of the Empire

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1405`

## leader_trait_demolisher_destiny_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_demolisher_destiny_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位领袖永远知道敌人的弱点集中火力打击，因此敌人惧怕于有朝一日会与之对垒。其引领的大型舰队以完美的效率和致命的精准在战场上大显神威，少有能够抵挡此种攻击的敌人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1329`

### English

Known for hitting hard and hitting where it hurts, our enemies should fear the day when this admiral joins the fray. Leading large fleets into battle with flawless efficiency and deadly acumen, few defensive perimeters stand a chance.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1406`

## leader_trait_destiny_engineer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_destiny_engineer&lang=en)

### 中文

维护指挥官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1330`

### English

Resilient Commander

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1407`

## leader_trait_destiny_engineer_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_destiny_engineer_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在太空战中，任何失误都可能是致命的。这位领袖坚持严格的维护规程来保证舰队时刻处于最佳状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1331`

### English

In space warfare, any mistake can turn deadly. This leader insists on strict maintenance routines to keep the fleet in excellent condition.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1408`

## leader_trait_guerilla_tactics

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_guerilla_tactics&lang=en)

### 中文

游击大师

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1332`

### English

Guerilla Tactician

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1409`

## leader_trait_guerilla_tactics_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_guerilla_tactics_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

大型舰队并不总是好用。这位舰队司令擅长于指挥小单位，以灵活机动胜过那些缓慢庞大的对手。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1333`

### English

Large fleets are not always the best tool for the job. This admiral is adept at commanding small units to outmaneuver slower or more numerous opponents.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1410`

## leader_trait_survivalist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_survivalist&lang=en)

### 中文

帝国之盾

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1334`

### English

Shield of the Empire

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1411`

## leader_trait_survivalist_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_survivalist_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

作为保护者的化身，这位舰队司令是前线真正的中流砥柱，击败一切胆敢进犯我们领土的敌人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1335`

### English

The embodiment of a protector, this admiral is a true mainstay on the front lines, obstructing any foes who dare to enter our territory.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1412`

## leader_trait_maven_of_war

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_maven_of_war&lang=en)

### 中文

战争构筑师

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1336`

### English

Architect of War

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1413`

## leader_trait_maven_of_war_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_maven_of_war_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

拥有这个头衔是一种巨大的荣誉。这样的舰队司令不仅拥有丰富的战争知识，而且在协调科学部门的同事方面也至关重要。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1337`

### English

Holding this title is a great honor. Such an admiral not only possesses vast knowledge of war, but is also a crucial link to colleagues in the science department.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1414`

## leader_trait_peacekeeper

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_peacekeeper&lang=en)

### 中文

和平守护者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1338`

### English

Peacekeeper

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1415`

## leader_trait_peacekeeper_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_peacekeeper_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位领袖意识到，为了维护和平，必须广泛撒网。最好的舰队司令领都在寻找那些异见分子、海盗，甚至是想利用贸易路线的小罪犯

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1339`

### English

This leader recognizes that to keep the peace one must cast a wide net. The best admirals are on the lookout for those dissidents, pirates, and petty criminals who aim to exploit our trade routes.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1416`

## leader_trait_supreme_warrior

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_supreme_warrior&lang=en)

### 中文

终极战士

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1341`

### English

Supreme Warrior

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1418`

## leader_trait_supreme_warrior_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_supreme_warrior_effect&lang=en)

### 中文

$t$地面战斗每经过§Y10§!天：\n$tt$直接击杀一支敌对陆军的概率：§Y15%§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1342`

### English

$t$Every §Y10§! Days in Ground Combat:\n$tt$Chance to Instantly Kill Hostile Army: §Y15%§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1419`

## leader_trait_supreme_warrior_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_supreme_warrior_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这位领袖是少数获得这一殊荣的指挥官之一。他们亲自率领军队，激励盟友，甚至压倒最强悍的敌人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1343`

### English

This leader is one of the few commanders to have attained this title. They lead their troops in person, inspiring allies and overwhelming even the toughest of foes.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1420`

## leader_trait_fortress_cracker

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_fortress_cracker&lang=en)

### 中文

堡垒克星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1344`

### English

Fortress Cracker

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1421`

## leader_trait_fortress_cracker_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_fortress_cracker_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

摧毁一座精心设计的防御设施需要力量、计谋和经验的组合。这位指挥官喜欢直面挑战，在这种别人可能会因为失败而放弃的难题中取胜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1345`

### English

It takes a combination of force, cunning, and experience to topple a well-designed defensive structure. This commander takes pleasure in the challenge and succeeds where others would give it up as a lost cause.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1422`

## leader_trait_chainbreaker

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_chainbreaker&lang=en)

### 中文

枷锁破除者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1346`

### English

Chainbreaker

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1423`

## leader_trait_chainbreaker_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_chainbreaker_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

任何感性生命不应被奴役，不应被当做其他人谋取利益的牲畜。这位领袖的名声在外，被奴役和被剥削的人受到他的召唤都会欣然拿起武器战斗。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1347`

### English

No sentient being should be enslaved and exploited for the profit of others. This leader's reputation precedes them, with the enslaved and oppressed gladly taking up arms when called.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1424`

## leader_trait_chainbreaker_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_chainbreaker_effect&lang=en)

### 中文

$t$进攻的星球上每个§Y$pop_cat_slave$§!有70%概率生成§G1§!陆军

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1348`

### English

$t$70% chance to spawn §G1§! Army per §Y$pop_cat_slave$§! on attacked planet

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1425`

## leader_trait_honored_warmaster

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_honored_warmaster&lang=en)

### 中文

荣耀老将

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1349`

### English

Honored Warmaster

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1426`

## leader_trait_honored_warmaster_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_honored_warmaster_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

荣耀老将是名声外在的指挥官。这位领袖会让人们感受到自豪和勇气，让他们渴望拿起武器加入进来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1350`

### English

Warmasters are commanders of renown. This leader instills pride and courage, making people eager to grab a weapon and enlist.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1427`

## leader_trait_honored_warmaster_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_honored_warmaster_effect&lang=en)

$PLANET_EFFECTS_WHILE_DEFENDING_TXT$\n$t$每§Y100§!£pop£§H非奴隶人口§!生成§G1§!防御部队

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1351`

### English

$PLANET_EFFECTS_WHILE_DEFENDING_TXT$\n$t$Spawn §G1§! Defensive Army per §Y100§! £pop£ §HNon-Slave Pops§!

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1428`

## leader_trait_pilferer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_pilferer&lang=en)

### 中文

掠夺军阀

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1352`

### English

Plundering Warlord

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1429`

## leader_trait_pilferer_effect

$ARMY_EFFECTS_TXT$：在§Y地面进攻战§!中§E获胜§!：\n$t$每个£district£§Y区划§!提供与之类别对应的资源数量：\n$tt$§G+100§!§Y基础资源§!£energy£ £minerals£ £food£\n$tt$§G+50§!§Y进阶资源§!£alloys£ £consumer_goods£\n$tt$§G+25§!§Y研究点数§!£physics£ £society£ £engineering£

### English

$ARMY_EFFECTS_TXT$ §EOn Winning§! §YGround Invasion§!:\n$t$Resources per associated £district£ §YDistrict§!:\n$tt$ §G+100§! §YBasic Resources§! £energy£ £minerals£ £food£\n$tt$ §G+50§! §YAdvanced Resources§! £alloys£ £consumer_goods£\n$tt$ §G+25§! §YResearch Points§! £physics£ £society£ £engineering£

## leader_trait_tzrynn_tithe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_tzrynn_tithe&lang=en)

### 中文

奇恩税

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1354`

### English

Tzynn Tithe

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1431`

## leader_trait_tzrynn_tithe_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_tzrynn_tithe_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

按照奇恩尼亚的惯例，任何冲突的赢家都会拿走输家最看重的三分之一。基雅·祖蒙保留了这一传统。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1355`

### English

On Tzynnia it is customary for the winner of any conflict to take a third of whatever the loser values most. Gia'Zumon keeps this tradition alive.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1432`

## leader_trait_pilferer_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_pilferer_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

星球越富有，战利品就越多，而这位领袖知道如何以最小代价夺取战利品。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1356`

### English

The richer the planet, the greater the spoils. This leader has a knack for extracting value at minimal cost.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1433`

## leader_trait_taskmaster

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_taskmaster&lang=en)

### 中文

大监工

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1357`

### English

Grand Taskmaster

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1434`

## leader_trait_taskmaster_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_taskmaster_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

监工不仅关注战斗策略，还关注所有士兵的日常琐事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1358`

### English

Taskmasters focus not only on strategy, but the minutia of every soldier's daily routine.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1435`

## leader_trait_spymaster

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_spymaster&lang=en)

### 中文

影中人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1359`

### English

Shadow Broker

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1436`

## leader_trait_spymaster_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_spymaster_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你说这位领袖是位间谍那就太小看人了，事实上其编织了一个由线人、走私者和暗杀者组成的网络。当需要悄悄完成某件事时，这将会成为一个非常有价值的资产。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1360`

### English

Far from a simple spy, this leader spins a web of informants, smugglers, and assassins. When something needs doing quietly, this can prove an enormous asset.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1437`

## leader_trait_totalitarian

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_totalitarian&lang=en)

### 中文

帝国掌礼官

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1361`

### English

Herald of the Empire

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1438`

## leader_trait_totalitarian_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_totalitarian_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

帝国掌礼官是一个代表着荣誉和声望的头衔，在国家中扮演着举足轻重的角色。这位外交官可以为宗主国和附属国之间提供相互信任的纽带。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1362`

### English

A title of honor and repute, the Herald is a master player in the great game of nations. Such diplomats provide a trusted link between an overlord and its vassals.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1439`

## building_paragon_memory_vaults_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_paragon_memory_vaults_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们社会一直保存着过去的英雄和杰出人物的故事。他们的知识和经验被数字化并收集在知识宝库中。一旦这栋建筑被摧毁，里面的数据便会荡然无存。                                     

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1514`

### English

Our society has always preserved the stories of heroes and prominent figures from the past. Their knowledge and experiences are digitized and gathered in the Vaults of Knowledge. Destroying this building will eradicate the data stored inside.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1597`

## building_paragon_memory_vaults_machine_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_paragon_memory_vaults_machine_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的单位经历的所有感觉和数据点都存储在这些知识宝库中。当一个新的自主单元被创建时，这个巨大的数据库可用于加速其学习。摧毁这个建筑将销毁里面储存的数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1515`

### English

Every sensation and data point experienced by our units is stored in these Vaults. This incredible database is used to accelerate learning when a new autonomous unit is created. Destroying this building will eliminate the data stored inside.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1598`

## building_paragon_memory_vaults_hive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abuilding_paragon_memory_vaults_hive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

所有的自主工蜂的记忆和感官印象都储存在知识库中，等待被植入新的工蜂中。一旦这栋建筑被摧毁，里面的数据便会荡然无存。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1516`

### English

The memories and sense impressions of every autonomous drone is stored in the Vaults of Knowledge, waiting to be imprinted in the minds of fresh drones. Destroying this building will eradicate the data stored inside.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1599`

## memory_vault_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amemory_vault_tooltip&lang=en)

领袖每获得一个§Y命定特质§!使§Y$MOD_LEADER_SPECIES_EXP_GAIN$§!和§Y$mod_country_unity_produces_mult$§!提高§G1%§!。\n维护费随领袖获得的§Y命定特质§!数量成比例提高

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1517`

### English

Increases §Y$MOD_LEADER_SPECIES_EXP_GAIN$§! and §Y$mod_country_unity_produces_mult$§! by §G1%§! for every §YDestiny Traits§! acquired by leaders.\nUpkeep scales for every §YDestiny Traits§! acquired by leaders

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1600`

一家私人医药公司雇用当地和国外的医疗专家以合理的费用提供服务。

## civic_memory_vault_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_memory_vault_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

杰出人才的知识、原则和经验被数字化后存储在智库中。公民们可以通过这座智慧和经验的源泉，向过去的模范学习。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1524`

### English

The knowledge, principles, and experiences of exceptional people are digitized and accumulated in the Vaults of Knowledge. Citizens can access this source of wisdom and experience to learn from the ghosts of the past.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1608`

## civic_tooltip_memory_vault_corporate_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_tooltip_memory_vault_corporate_effects&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1525`

Can build unique £building£§Y $building_paragon_memory_vaults$§!\n£building£§Y$building_paragon_memory_vaults$§! produce $r_unity$ and $r_trade$ based on §YDestiny Traits§! acquired.\nThe £building£ §Y$building_commercial_forum$§! $megacorp_civic_branch_office_building_effect$

## civic_memory_vault_corporate_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_memory_vault_corporate_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

杰出人才的知识、原则和经验被数字化后存储在智库中。公民们可以通过这座智慧和经验的源泉，向过去的模范学习。当然，要交钱。

The knowledge, principles, and experiences of exceptional people are digitized and accumulated in the Vaults of Knowledge. Citizens can access this source of wisdom and experience to learn from the ghosts of the past. For a price, of course.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1612`

## civic_memory_vault_machine_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_memory_vault_machine_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该机械智能以对数据和经验进行数字化存储为目标开发，并能从中自分析、自学习、自应用、自升级。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1530`

### English

This machine intelligence was developed with the goal of digitizing and storing data and experiences in order to analyze, learn from, and use it for its own improvement.

## civic_memory_vault_hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_memory_vault_hive&lang=en)

### 中文

神经元宝库

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1532`

### English

Neural Vaults

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1619`

## civic_memory_vault_hive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_memory_vault_hive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该蜂巢专注于内省，从星系中的自主工蜂中进行学习。它能通过对脑组织的存储和溶解提取其中的记忆，并将部分记忆植入新产生的工蜂中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1533`

### English

This hive is focused on introspection and learning from the lives of autonomous drones in the galaxy. It extracts memories through the storage and dissolution of brain tissue, through which it can implement partial memories into a freshly prepared drone.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1620`

## civic_memory_vault_wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_memory_vault_wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

$civic_memory_vault_hive$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1534`

### English

$civic_memory_vault_hive$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1621`

## civic_memory_vault_wilderness_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_memory_vault_wilderness_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这片内省的荒野从自主生命形式的生活和教训中汲取灵感，并将它们的记忆融入到其所有共生体共享的本能之中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1535`

### English

This introspective Wilderness finds its muse in the lives and lessons of autonomous lifeforms, incorporating their memories as instincts shared among all its symbionts.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1622`

## civic_tooltip_crusader_spirit_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_tooltip_crusader_spirit_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$commander_plural_with_icon$§!初始拥有§Y$leader_trait_crusader$§!特质\n仅限使用§Y解放战争§!政策

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1536`

### English

§Y$commander_plural_with_icon$§! start with the §Y$leader_trait_crusader$§! Trait\nCan only use §YLiberation Wars§! policy

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1624`

## civic_crusader_spirit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_crusader_spirit&lang=en)

### 中文

十字军之魂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1537`

### English

Crusader Spirit

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1625`

## civic_crusader_spirit_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_crusader_spirit_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

缺乏向导的灵魂是脆弱的，很容易屈从于腐败，陷入堕落深渊。而这个社会通过积极的传教为大众带来“启蒙”，为人们构筑起了一道自我防御的铠甲。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1538`

### English

The lack of guidance creates weak minds - which easily succumb to corruption and plunge into decadence. This society has assumed the self-imposed mantle of bringing "enlightenment" to the masses through aggressive proselytizing.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1626`

## civic_tooltip_crusader_spirit_corporate_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_tooltip_crusader_spirit_corporate_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$commander_plural_with_icon$§!初始拥有§Y$leader_trait_corsair$§!特质\n对其他帝国拥有“§Y$casus_belli_cb_pirate_raid$§!”的宣战理由\n£building£§Y$building_military_contractors$§!$megacorp_civic_branch_office_building_effect$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1539`

### English

§Y$commander_plural_with_icon$§! start with the §Y$leader_trait_corsair$§! trait\nHas the '§Y$casus_belli_cb_pirate_raid$§!' casus belli on all other empires.\nThe £building£ §Y$building_military_contractors$§! $megacorp_civic_branch_office_building_effect$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1628`

## civic_crusader_spirit_corporate

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_crusader_spirit_corporate&lang=en)

### 中文

私掠许可

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1540`

### English

Letters of Marque

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1629`

## civic_crusader_spirit_corporate_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_crusader_spirit_corporate_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了打击太空海盗和保卫边界，这个寰宇企业自己也利用臭名昭著的海盗作为他们舰队的军事顾问和指挥官。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1541`

### English

To combat space piracy and safeguard its borders, this megacorporation utilizes notorious pirates as military advisors and commanders for their fleets.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1630`

## casus_belli_cb_pirate_raid

### 中文

发动战争，以袭击和掠夺敌人。该战争目标可以掠夺目标的$r_energy$和$r_minerals$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1546`

## civic_task_delegation_corporate_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_task_delegation_corporate_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该寰宇企业在一个高度优化和强大的委派系统上运作。这种应对方案意味着每个任务都是由最合格的工人来完成。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1548`

### English

This megacorporation operates on a highly optimized and robust system of delegation. Such an approach means that a given task is always performed by the most qualified worker.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1638`

## civic_private_healthcare_corporate_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_private_healthcare_corporate_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个强大的国家必须是一个健康的国家。该寰宇企业通过实验性药物、高度私有化的医疗保健，以及积极的营销策略始终维持经济的强健有力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1551`

### English

A strong nation is a healthy nation. This megacorporation maintains a robust economy through experimental drugs, highly privatized clinics, and aggressive marketing campaigns.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1642`

## civic_machine_upkeep_twister_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_machine_upkeep_twister_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由这种机器智能生产的单元配备了自主决策软件。与旧型号相比，这些设备有更高的效率和更好的效果，但也需要更大的动力输入。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1554`

### English

Units produced by this machine intelligence come equipped with autonomous decision-making software. More efficient and effective than older models, these units nevertheless require greater power input.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1646`

## civic_hive_upkeep_twister

## civic_hive_upkeep_twister_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_hive_upkeep_twister_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这个群落中进化出的高代谢工蜂拥有更多的自主权。这些工蜂在强化决策和局势评估的同时，要求摄入更多的可消化营养物质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1557`

### English

The hypermetabolic drones evolved in this colony possess increased autonomy. While decision-making and situation assessment are enhanced, these drones require an increased intake of digestible materials.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1651`

## civic_tooltip_hive_upkeep_twister_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_tooltip_hive_upkeep_twister_effects&lang=en)

将§Y1§!基础$r_unity$领袖维护费替换为§Y2§!$r_food$、$r_minerals$或§Y1§!$r_alloys$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1558`

### English

Change §Y1§! Base $r_unity$ Leader Upkeep to §Y2§! $r_food$, $r_minerals$ or §Y1§! $r_alloys$

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1652`

## civic_wilderness_upkeep_twister

## civic_wilderness_upkeep_twister_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_wilderness_upkeep_twister_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这个荒野之中，一个独特且能力超群的共生体演化分支已经形成，它们以更快的速度发展成为领导者，但也因此需要更高的代谢维持。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1560`

### English

A uniquely capable clade of symbionts has evolved within this Wilderness, developing more quickly as leaders in exchange for higher metabolic upkeep.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1654`

### 中文

过去的传奇英雄激励着这个文明的每个新世代去实现非凡的成就。作为榜样，政府的官员整践行着这些理想。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1562`

### English

The legendary heroes of the past inspire each new generation to achieve the extraordinary. As role models, government officials embody these ideals.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1657`

### 中文

只有精英中的精英才配得上最好的东西。其他所有人都要为统治阶级和他们的走狗提供服务。

### 中文

侵略性野生生物在这片土地上搜寻猎物，定居点需要提升安保来遏制这一威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1580`

### English

Aggressive wildlife scours the land on the hunt for prey. Settlements need increased protection to curtail this threat.

### 中文

侵略性植物在这片大陆上蔓生，寻找着猎物。需要指派安保人员确保这些过度繁茂的植物在可控范围之内。

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_space_ship_graveyard_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

大量老旧的舰船散落在地表上，妨碍了该地区可能的采矿作业。尽管如此，它们提供了一种容易获得的合金来源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1584`

### English

Old ships litter the planet, inhibiting the potential mining operations in the area. Though, they provide an easily accessible source of alloys.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1681`

## d_fallen_orbital_shipyard

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_fallen_orbital_shipyard&lang=en)

### 中文

坠毁船坞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1585`

### English

Fallen Shipyard

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1682`

## d_fallen_orbital_shipyard_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_fallen_orbital_shipyard_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一座古代的轨道造船厂的破碎残骸散落在行星表面。这里非常适合研究和开采，其扭曲的船体和休眠的机械中依然蕴藏着丰富的可回收材料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1586`

### English

The shattered remains of an ancient orbital shipyard strewn across the planetary surface. Ripe for study and extraction, is twisted hull and dormant machinery remain rich with salvageable materials.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1683`

## d_pulsar_scarred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_pulsar_scarred&lang=en)

### 中文

满目疮痍的大地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1587`

### English

Scarred Land

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1684`

## d_pulsar_scarred_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_pulsar_scarred_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

满目疮痍的大地和废墟遍地的焦土难以进行利用，除非能够驾驭持续脉冲星的潜在能量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1588`

### English

Scarred lands and burnt-out wastelands are impossible to use outside of harnessing the potential power from the constant pulsar flares.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1685`

## d_ancient_facilities

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_ancient_facilities&lang=en)

### 中文

远古设施

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1589`

### English

Ancient Facilities

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1686`

## d_ancient_facilities_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_ancient_facilities_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些远古设施包含着关于脉冲星、护盾科技和从最危险的恒星中提取能量的研究数据 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1590`

### English

These ancient facilities contain research data on pulsars, shielding technology, and extracting energy from the most dangerous suns. 

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1687`

## molten_mineral_rivers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amolten_mineral_rivers&lang=en)

### 中文

熔矿河川

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1591`

### English

Molten Mineral Rivers

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1688`

## molten_mineral_rivers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amolten_mineral_rivers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该行星随处发生着火山喷发，大量的熔岩在内地壳中汩汩流淌，有助于提高行星上矿物处理设施的运转。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1592`

### English

This planet is plagued by frequent volcanic eruptions and a high amount of molten lava flowing through its inner crust, which is instrumental for mineral processing facilities operating on the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1689`

## hostile_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahostile_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

危机四伏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1593`

### English

Hostile Planet

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1690`

## hostile_planet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahostile_planet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星上的动植物都演化成了一种“吃或被吃”的捕食形态。它们提供了大量的营养物质，但也会积极寻找其他生物作为食物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1594`

### English

On this planet, flora and fauna evolved into a predatory eat-or-be-eaten scheme. They provide a lot of nutrients but will also actively hunt out other living creatures for sustenance.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1691`

## ship_junkyard

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aship_junkyard&lang=en)

### 中文

舰船坟场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1595`

### English

Ship Junkyard

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1692`

## ship_junkyard_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aship_junkyard_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星充斥着老旧废弃的舰船和一座坠毁的轨道船坞。它是一处打捞珍贵金属和老旧科技的绝佳场所。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1596`

### English

This planet is filled with old derelict ships and a fallen orbital shipyard. It makes for a great place to scavenge for precious metals as well as old technology.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1693`

## pulsar_frying_pan

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apulsar_frying_pan&lang=en)

### 中文

中子狂耀

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1597`

### English

Pulsar Flares

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1694`

## pulsar_frying_pan_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apulsar_frying_pan_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星的一个半球被邻近的脉冲星侵蚀，使这一面成为了无法居住的废土，但却能使我们与能量相关科技的研究设施以极高效率运作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_2_l_simp_chinese.yml:1598`

### English

Half of the planet is battered by a nearby pulsar star. It turned this surface into an inhabitable wasteland, but it allows our research facilities to work on energy-related tech in a vastly accelerated manner.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_2_l_english.yml:1695`
